CORPORATE PROFILE
公司概況
China Foods Limited (“China Foods” or the “Company” and together with its
subsidiaries, the “Group”), a subsidiary of COFCO Corporation (“COFCO”) and the
only focused beverage platform of COFCO, is listed on the main board of The Stock
Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (Stock Code: 506).


The Company mainly manufactures, distributes, markets and sells the Coca-Cola
series products at nineteen provincial-level administrative regions in the PRC through
COFCO Coca-Cola Beverages Limited (“COFCO Coca-Cola”), a 65%-owned joint-venture
with The Coca-Cola Company. COFCO Coca-Cola offers twenty-four brands of products
in eleven major types of beverages which include sparkling drink, juice, water, milk
drink, energy drink, tea, coffee, functional nutrition drink, sports drink, plant-based
protein drink and alcoholic drink.


中國食品有限公司（簡稱「中國食品」或「本公司」
                      ，連同其附屬公司統稱「本集團」）為中
糧集團有限公司（「中糧」）的附屬公司及中糧唯一的專業化飲料業務平台，並於香港聯
合交易所有限公司主板上市（股份代號：506）
                     。


本公司目前主要通過與可口可樂公司合作成立並持有65%權益的中糧可口可樂飲料有
限公司（簡稱「中糧可口可樂」）在國內19個省級行政區域從事生產、配送、推廣和銷售
可口可樂系列產品，向消費者提供包括汽水、果汁、水、乳飲料、能量飲料、茶、咖啡、
功能型營養素飲料、運動飲料、植物蛋白飲料及酒精飲料等11大品類、24個品牌的產
品。

                                                                                    2021年大事 記


2020
 China Foods was granted the “Zhong Liang Bai Zhan Award”（忠良百戰獎）by COFCO for its outstanding
 performance in 2020
 中國食品因2020年的優秀業績表現榮獲中糧集團頒發的「忠良百戰獎」




COFCO has set up the “Zhong Liang Bai Zhan Award” to reward teams with the most outstanding performance
each year. In 2020, China Foods overcame the impact of the pandemic, maintained the stability of its operation
presence, and achieved improvement in its        y. With its performance highly recognised by COFCO, China
Foods was granted the “Zhong Liang Bai Zhan Award”.
中糧集團設立「忠良百戰獎」              ，獎勵每年業績最突出的團隊。中國食品2020年克服疫情影響，保證業務規模的穩
定，實現盈利能力的提升，業績表現得到中糧集團的高度認可，被授予「忠良百戰獎」                                         。




 COFCO Coca-Cola got its Coca-Cola Global “Candler Cup”
 中糧可口可樂首次入圍可口可樂全球「坎德勒杯」大獎的決賽
                                                                  l
                                                                                2021

In 2021, COFCO Coca-Cola was shortlisted for the            time in the Candler Cup       of the Coca-Cola’s Best
Bottling Group with its leading pace in digital advancement and the growth rate of business scale. This marked
the      time that COFCO Coca-Cola entered the            in 21 years since its establishment, which          the
comprehensive recognition of its growth,            y, innovation and sustainable development by the Coca-Cola’s
system.
2021年，中糧可口可樂憑藉領先系統的數字化推進步伐和業務規模增長速度，首次入圍可口可樂系統最佳裝瓶
集團大獎Candeler Cup的決賽。這是中糧可口可樂成立21年以來首次入圍決賽，體現了可口可樂系統對公司增長
性、盈利性、創新性以及可持續發展等多方面的綜合認可。

    MAJOR EVENTS IN 2021 2 0 2 1 年 大 事 記




    2021
     COFCO Coca-Cola’s self-developed “Coke GO” system won the Coca-Cola Global Best Commercial Leadership
     Awards
     中糧可口可樂自主開發的「可樂GO」系統榮獲可口可樂全球最佳商務領導力大獎




    In 2021, the Coca-Cola system set up a special award for excellent digital commercial capabilities for the first
    time, to appraise the bottling group’s contribution to improving digital leadership through the evaluation of digital
    core capabilities and project innovation, combined with the assessment of multi-dimensional indicators such as
    system revenue, product distribution, transaction frequency and market share. COFCO Coca-Cola got the first
    place in the competition with its leading digital concept and outstanding practice results of the “Coke GO” project.
    2021年，可口可樂系統首次針對卓越數字化商務能力設立專項獎項，通過對數字化核心能力和項目創新性的評
    估，結合系統收入、產品鋪貨、交易頻次和市場份額等多維度指標的考核，評價裝瓶集團在提升數字化領導力方面
    做出的貢獻。中糧可口可樂憑藉領先的數字化理念和優秀的「可樂GO」項目實踐結果，在評比中摘取桂冠。



     COFCO Coca-Cola Beijing Plant, as Coca-Cola’s first plant in China, celebrated its 40th anniversary
     中糧可口可樂北京廠作為可口可樂在中國首家工廠，迎來40周年紀念




    COFCO Coca-Cola Beijing Plant, the first bottling plant established by Coca-Cola in China, celebrated its 40th
    anniversary on 15 April 2021. COFCO Coca-Cola held a new journey launch event themed “pursuing development
    with innovation and reform, taking on the momentum to open a new phase”, to cheer for achieving the 14th
    Five-Year Plan of COFCO Coca-Cola and the Beijing Plant. Mr. Luan Richeng, the President of COFCO, attended
    the meeting.
    中糧可口可樂北京廠是可口可樂在中國建立的首家裝瓶廠，2021年4月15日迎來40周年紀念。中糧可口可樂以「創
    新改革謀發展、乘勢而上開新局」為主題舉辦新征程啟動活動，為中糧可口可樂及北京廠的十四五規劃加油鼓勁。
    中糧集團總裁欒日成先生出席會議。




6   CHINA FOODS LIMITED ANNUAL REPORT 2021
                                                                           MAJOR EVENTS IN 2021 2 0 2 1 年 大 事 記




 COFCO Coca-Cola vigorously advanced the development of new products, and launched 91 new items throughout
 the year
 中糧可口可樂大力推動新品發展，全年上市91個全新單品




In 2021, COFCO Coca-Cola, with its commitment to the customer-oriented business philosophy, and an aim
to meet the diversified product needs of consumers, launched a total of 91 new products, among which each
of COSTA, Minute Maid peach juice （ 美 汁 源 汁 汁 桃 桃 ）and Light Soda AHHA （ 輕 汽 水 小 宇 宙 AHHA）and
Chunyue functional water, generated a revenue of over RMB100 million during the year.
2021年，中糧可口可樂秉承「以客戶為目標」的經營理念，為滿足消費者多樣化的產品需求，共上市全新單品91
個，其中COSTA咖世家、美汁源汁汁桃桃和輕汽水小宇宙AHHA、純悅功能水等產品當年收入均過億。

 China Foods’ first domestic source water product, Yuehuo Emeishan Water, was officially launched
 中國食品首款國內水源水產品悅活峨眉山水正式上市




On 8 April, China Foods’ first domestic source water product, COFCO Yuehuo Emeishan Mineral Water, was
officially launched. Mr. Chen Lang, Vice President of COFCO Group and the chairman of the board of directors
of the Company, Mr. Zhang Zhigang, former Vice Minister of the Ministry of Commerce and former Chairman
of China General Chamber of Commerce, Mr. Zhao Yali, Chairman of China Beverage Industry Association, and
Mr. Qing Lijun, the managing director of China Foods attended the launch ceremony. COFCO Yuehuo Company,
a subsidiary of China Foods, signed a strategic cooperation agreement with Emeishan Municipal Government to
jointly promote the premium Emeishan mineral water under the theme of “Enjoying Life with Natural Taste”（悅
享生活品味自然）.
4月8日，中國食品首款國內水源水產品—中糧悅活峨眉山礦泉水正式上市，中糧集團副總裁兼本公司董事會主席
陳朗先生，商務部原副部長、中國商業聯合會原會長張志剛先生，中國飲料工業協會名譽理事長趙亞利先生、中國
食品董事總經理慶立軍先生出席發佈儀式。中國食品下屬中糧悅活公司與峨眉山市政府簽署戰略合作協議，以「悅
享生活品味自然」為主題共同推廣峨眉山優質礦泉水。

     19 Bottling Plants of COFCO Coca-Cola were awarded as the Excellent Enterprises on energy and water saving
     by the industry association
     中糧可口可樂19家裝瓶廠榮獲行業協會頒發的節能節水企業稱號




    All of 19 bottling plants of COFCO Coca-Cola have achieved outstanding results on energy saving and emission
    reduction, and all of them were awarded the title of Excellent Enterprises on Water and Energy Saving in China’s
    Beverage Industry under Lucid Waters and Lush Mountains Cup, and gained a widely recognition. In recognition
    of the outstanding contributions made in the field of public welfare research, the China Beverage Industry Association
    also granted COFCO Coca-Cola with “2020 Public Welfare Research Contribution Award”.
    中糧可口可樂全部19家裝瓶廠憑藉在節能減排上取得的顯著成效，獲評入圍「綠水青山杯」中國飲料工業節水、節
    能優秀企業獲獎名單，贏得了行業的廣泛認可。為表彰在公益研究領域所做出的卓越貢獻，中國飲料工業協會同
    時授予中糧可口可樂「2020年公益研究貢獻獎」                          。

     toward the second Centenary Goal.                                           新征程。

     The past year also marks a ground-breaking year for China Foods.            2021年對於中國食品也是有里程碑意義的

University with a Bachelor’s degree in Economics and a Master’s degree in Business Administration, respectively.

     unit and procurement department of the Company. From October 2016 to April 2019, he served as the deputy
     general manager of China Agri-Industries Holdings Limited, once a company listed in Hong Kong, and a director
     of the preparatory office of the COFCO Yingkou Lingang Grain, Oil and Food Industrial Park.

     Mr. Chen has extensive experience in the research and practices of operating management of national policies,
     industrial development, international trade, production and processing, brand marketing and technological innovation
     in the field of grain, oil and food. Mr. Chen also serves as the Vice President of the China National Vegetable Oil
     Association, the Vice President of the Oil Chapter of the Chinese Cereals and Oils Association, and a member of
     the National Cereals and Oils Standardisation Technical Committee.

     Mr. Chen graduated from Peking University with a Bachelor of Arts degree in law, and from Tsinghua University
     with a degree of Executive Master of Business Administration. Mr. Chen also held a PhD degree in Economics
     from Institute of Population and Labour Economics, Chinese Academy of Social Sciences.

     陳剛先生
     陳先生，50歲，於2021年8月獲委任為非執行董事。陳先生於1994年加入中糧集團有限公司，現為中糧集團有限
     公司對外合作部總監兼保障供應部總經理。陳先生曾任中糧黃海糧油工業（山東）有限公司常務副總經理及中糧北
     海糧油工業（天津）有限公司總經理。陳先生於2008年至2016年期間曾歷任本集團的多個管理職位，包括本公司副
     總經理、總經理助理、總法律顧問、休閒食品品類部總經理、廚房食品品類部總經理及採購部總經理等。於2016年

     準化技術委員會委員。

                                            • Relevant social responsibility issues that cover corporate governance, environmental protection, interests
                                              and rights of employees, supply chain management, customer responsibility and community investment
                                              were identified by collating feedbacks from daily communication with stakeholders, and collecting major
                                              issues related to the Company’s operation and management, with reference to the international and
                                              domestic sustainable development guidelines and industry practices based on the ESG Reporting Guide
                                              of the Stock Exchange.
                            ISSUES          • 通過整理與利益相關方的日常溝通反饋，收集與本公司經營管理相關的主要議題，在香港聯交所《ESG
                                              報告指引》的基礎上參考國際國內可持續發展指引及同業實踐，識別出覆蓋公司治理、環境保護、員
                        IDENTIFICATION        工權益、供應鏈管理、客戶責任、社區投資等方面相關社會責任議題。
                           議題識別



                                            • In the form of questionnaires, the management, customers, employees, suppliers and other major
                                              stakeholders of Company were invited to evaluate the materiality of each issue from their own perspective,
                                              and a total of 131 questionnaires were collected.
                                            • 採取問卷方式，邀請公司管理層、客戶、員工、供應商等主要利益相關方 從自身角度評估各議題的重
                                              要程度，共收集問卷 131 份。
                            ISSUES
                          EVALUATION
                           議題評估


                                            • Based on the two dimensions of “Importance to stakeholders” and “Importance to the Company ”,
                                              materiality of issues is ranked by the issue matrix. External experts were invited to participate in the
                                              discussion, and 32 material issues were identified as the priority areas of the Company’s ESG manage-
                                              ment work, and disclosed in the Report as key items.
                                            • 基於“ 議題對利益相關方的重要性 ”與“ 議題對本公司的重要性 ”兩個維度，通過重要性議題矩陣對
                                              議題重要性進行排序。邀請外部專家參與討論，確認了 32 個重要性議題作為本公司 ESG 管理工作優

     2.   GREEN DEVELOPMENT                                                 2.   綠色發展
          Today, when environmental problems are becoming                        在 環 境 問 題 日 益 凸 顯 的 今 天，綠 色
          increasingly prominent, the green and low-carbon production            低 碳 生 產 已 成 為 實 生 態 文 明 建 設、

2.   GREEN DEVELOPMENT (continued)                                2.    綠色發展（續）
     2.1 Emissions and waste management (continued)                     2.1   排放物及廢棄物管理（續）

         Case: COFCO Coca-Cola Beijing Plant phased out fuel trucks
         案例：中糧可口可樂北京廠淘汰燃油貨車

         In 2021, in order to reduce the exhaust emissions of fuel vehicles and lower the impact on the
         atmospheric environment, COFCO Coca-Cola Beijing Plant replaced 40 4.2-meter diesel-fueled van
         trucks with new energy electric trucks. After such replacement, carbon dioxide emissions will be
         reduced by more than 330 tonnes on an annual basis; and various types of emissions can be
         effectively reduced. In the future, COFCO Coca-Cola Beijing Plant plans to gradually phase out more
         outmoded fuel trucks with high emission, to achieve a more environmental-friendly logistics and
         transportation system, subject to the freight capacity.
         2021年，為降低燃油汽車尾氣排放，減少對大氣環境影響，中糧可口可樂北京廠將40輛4.2米廂式柴油貨
         車，替換成新能源電動貨車。更換電動車後，每年可減少二氧化碳排放330餘噸，並有效減排各類排放物
         氣體。在未來，中糧可口可樂北京廠計劃在保證貨運能力的要求下，逐步淘汰更多老舊高排放燃油貨車，
         以實現更加綠色的物流運輸系統。

                goals for production safety responsibility and put forward corresponding measures on three
                aspects of “continuous reduction of accident risks, parallel development of mechanisation,

                全文化」三方面制定了安全生產責任目標，並提出配套措施。

 Goals for production safety responsibility   Supporting measures taken to achieve the goals
 安全生產責任目標                                     為實現目標所開展的配套措施
                                              •    Carried out special inspections on stability-related risks,
                                                   hazardous chemicals, electrical safety, and the safety of
                                                   leased warehouses, and check and test on the
                                                   comprehensiveness and effectiveness of major safety risk
                                                   management and control measures, to make continuous
                                                   improvement, and consolidate risk prevention and control
                                                   capabilities;
                                              •    開展涉穩風險、危險化學品、電氣安全、外租庫安全等專項排
                                                   查，檢查並檢驗主要安全風險管控措施的全面性與有效性，推動
                                                   持續改進，夯實風險防控能力；

                                              •    Intensified the on-site supervision and inspection, conducted
                                                   monthly review of online video, and organised mutual
                                                   inspections of videos in plants to follow up any rectifications;
                                              •    加大現場「四不兩直」監督檢查力度，持續每月線上視頻倒查，以
                                                   及組織工廠視頻互查，跟進整改落實；

                                              •    Improved the 37 sheets of Hidden Hazard Identification
                                                   Standard for Industry Chain（《產業鏈隱患排查標準》）,
                                                   which covers all places, all equipment, all operations, and
                                                   all personnel in production areas, logistics areas, auxiliary
                                                   areas, leased warehouses, and construction projects. The
                                                   bottling plants, pursuant to the Hidden Hazard Identification
                                                   Standard for Industry Chain（《產業鏈隱患排查標準》）,
                                                   organised comprehensive inspections, special inspections,
                                                   seasonal inspections, daily inspections, etc., so as to identify
                                                   and handle hidden dangers in a timely manner;
                                              •    完善《產業鏈隱患排查標準》37張表單，隱患排查覆蓋生產區、
                                                   物流區、輔助區、外租庫、建設項目等所有場所、所有設備、所
                                                   有作業、所有人員。裝瓶廠根據最新版《產業鏈隱患排查標準》
                                                   組織綜合檢查、專項檢查、季節性檢查、日常檢查等，及時發現
                                                   並治理隱患；

                                              •    19 plants arranged emergency drills for eight major dangers,
                                                   including falling from heights, poisoning and suffocation,
                                                   electric shock, mechanical injury, fire, vehicle injury, lifting
                                                   injury, and object strike. Confined space operations are
                                                   equipped with emergency rescue system and emergency
                                                   escape breathing device, to enhance emergency rescue
                                                   capability and improve safety management;
                                              •    19家工廠組織高處墜落、中毒窒息、觸電、機械傷害、火災、車
                                                   輛傷害、起重傷害、物體打擊等八大類重大危險應急演練，配備
                                                   有限空間作業應急救援系統以及應急逃生呼吸器，增強應急救
                                                   援能力，提升安全管理水平；

                                                   •    Established on-site emergency response plans for eight
                                                        major risks to dispose the major risk emergency in a
                                                        standard and unified way, while promoting grass-level
                                                        enterprises to increase the frequency of drills, improve the
                                                        quality of drills, so as to continuously improve their
                                                        comprehensive emergency response capabilities. More than
                                                        70 safety emergency drills were carried out in 2021. An
                                                        amount of RMB one million was invested for the advanced,
                                                        safe and convenient emergency equipment and rescue
                                                        equipment in 2021.
                                                   •    建立八類重大風險現場應急處置方案，推動重大風險應急處置
                                                        的標準化、統一化。同時，推動基層企業加大演練頻次，提高演
                                                        練質量，不斷提升綜合應急處置能力。2021年開展安全應急演
                                                        練70多次。2021年投入近百萬元配備具備先進性、安全性、便
                                                        捷性的應急裝救援備。

      Parallel development of mechanisation,       •    10 plants were select to implement “safety and intelligence”
      automation, informatisation, intelligence         projects, including electronic locks in confined spaces and
      「四化」    （機械化、自動化、信息化、智能                           electronic locks added to the entrance for high-altitude
      化）並進                                              operations to improve the safety level of dangerous
                                                        operations; intelligent video systems introduced in logistics
                                                        areas to actively identify unsafe behaviors, monitor and
                                                        correct hidden dangers such as the deviation of workers in
                                                        the logistics area from the prescribed route, not wearing
                                                        safety helmets, and not wearing reflective vests, so as to
                                                        achieve early identification, timely correction, and timely
                                                        treatment. At present, the project has been officially
                                                        launched, with selected pilot enterprises to carry out the
                                                        preparation and planning before implementation, and will be
                                                        advanced as planned;
                                                   •    選擇10家工廠推行「安全智能化」項目，包括有限空間電子鎖、
                                                        高處作業進入點增加電子鎖，提升危險作業安全水平；物流區域
                                                        引入智能視頻系統，主動識別不安全行為，監控並糾正物流區域
                                                        作業人員未按規定路線行走、未戴安全帽、未穿反光背心等隱
                                                        患，做到早識別、早糾正、早治理。目前該項目已正式啟動，並已
                                                        選擇試點企業開展實施前的組織與策劃，後續按計劃推動；

                                                   •    In 2021, 10 bottling plants were organised to build a
                                                        dangerous operation management information system, so as
                                                        to realise online approval of the entire dangerous operations,
                                                        implement monitoring and video review, and eliminate
                                                        hidden dangers of accidents.
                                                   •    2021年組織10家裝瓶廠建設危險作業管理信息化系統，實現危
                                                        險作業全程在線審批、實施監控與視頻倒查，消除事故隱患。

     including but not limited to the Criminal Law of the People’s         華人民共和國刑法》 、
                                                                                     《中華人民共和
     Republic of China（《中華人民共和國刑法》）, the Law of the                        國反不正當競爭法》 、
                                                                                     《關於禁止商業

7.   COMMUNITY INVESTMENT (continued)                          7.    社區投資（續）
     7.2 Supporting community development with                       7.2   支持社區發展助力公益慈善
         contribution to public welfare (continued)                        （續）
         Disaster assistance   China Foods (COFCO Coca-Cola) owned 19 bottling plants covering 81% of
         災害援助                  China and 51% of its population. In cases of natural disaster, the plants’
                               strong marketing network and logistical resources are harnessed to bring
                               essentials such as drinking water to where it is vitally needed. Among them,
                               the sudden heavy rainfall disaster in Zhengzhou, Henan Province in August
                               this year, seriously affected local people’s water consumption. In response
                               to the call of the country and COFCO, COFCO Yuehuo, a subsidiary of China
                               Foods activated the emergency relief mechanism, and summoned all forces
                               to deliver a total of 360,000 bottles of Yuehuo natural mineral water (with
                               a value of RMB1,260,000) to Zhengzhou on August 23, safeguarding the
                               living needs of victims in disaster-stricken areas. In 2021, the Group
                               responded to the “Clean Water in 24 Hours” rescue mechanism on 6
                               occasions, providing more than 689,396 bottles of water to victims and
                               rescuers in disaster-stricken areas.
                               中國食品（中糧可口可樂）擁有19家裝瓶廠，覆蓋了81%的國土面積和51%
                               的中國大陸人口。基於強大的營銷網絡和物流資源，在同胞有難之時，及時
                               將飲用水運抵重災區，幫助災民緩解無水困境。其中，在今年8月河南鄭州
                               突發強降雨災害後，當地民眾用水受到嚴重影響，中國食品旗下中糧悅活，
                               快速響應國家和中糧號召，緊急啟動應急救援機制，在第一時間，調集各方
                               力量，於8月23日將共計36萬瓶悅活天然礦泉水（價值126萬元）運抵鄭州，
                               為災區民眾生活需要提供了切實的保障。2021年，本公司共響應「淨水24小
                               時」救援機制6次，第一時間為災區同胞和救援人員支援689,396瓶飲用水。




                               The Company provided drinking water to Zhengzhou, Henan
                               本公司向河南鄭州提供飲用水物資保障

3   SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                  3   主要會計政策
    3.1 Principles of consolidation and equity accounting                3.1   合併及權益會計原則
         i.   Subsidiaries                                                     i. 附屬公司
              Subsidiaries are all entities (including structured                 附屬公司指本集團對其
              entities) over which the Group has control. The                     擁有控制權的所有實體
              Group controls an entity where the Group is                         （包括結構性實體）   。當本
              exposed to, or has rights to, variable returns                      集團因參與該實體而承
              from its involvement with the entity and has the                    擔可變回報之風險或享
              ability to affect those returns through its power                   有可變回報之權利，並有
              to direct the activities of the entity. Subsidiaries                能力透過其指揮該實體
              are fully consolidated from the date on which                       活動之權力影響該等回
              control is transferred to the Group. They are                       報，則 本 集 團 控 制 該 實
              deconsolidated from the date that control ceases.                   體。附屬公司自控制權轉
                                                                                  移至本集團當日起全面
                                                                                  綜合入賬。於控制權終止
                                                                                  當日起停止合併入賬。

              Inter-company transactions, balances and                            集團內公司間之交易、結
              unrealised gains on transactions between group                      餘及未變現收益均予以
              companies are eliminated. Unrealised losses are                     對銷。未變現虧損亦予以
              also eliminated unless the transaction provides                     對銷，除非交易有證據顯
              evidence of an impairment of the transferred                        示所轉讓資產出現減值。
              asset. Accounting policies of subsidiaries have                     附屬公司的會計政策已
              been changed where necessary to ensure                              在有需要時作出改變，以
              consistency with the policies adopted by the                        確保與本集團所採納的
              Group.                                                              政策一致。

              Non-controlling interests in the results and                        非控股權益於附屬公司
              equity of subsidiaries are shown separately in                      業績及權益中分別於綜
              the consolidated statement of profit or loss,                       合損益表、全面收益表、
              statement of comprehensive income, statement                        權益變動表及財務狀況
              of changes in equity and statement of financial                     表內呈列。
              position respectively.

                ii.    Associates                                                      ii.    聯營公司

                       Group holds between 20% and 50% of the                                 實 體。本 集 團 一 般 持 有

                       adjusted thereafter to recognise the Group’s                           調整，以確認本集團應佔
                       share of the post-acquisition profits or losses of                     被投資方的收購事項後
                       the investee in profit or loss, and the Group’s                        損益及本集團應佔被投
                       share of movements in other comprehensive                              資方的其他全面收益變
                       income of the investee in other comprehensive                          動。已收或應收聯營公司
                       income. Dividends received or receivable from                          股息乃確認為投資賬面
                       associates are recognised as a reduction in the                        值之減少。
                       carrying amount of the investment.

                       Where the Group’s share of losses in an equity-                        倘本集團應佔以權益入

                       unsecured long-term receivables, the Group                             括任何其他無抵押長期
                       does not recognise further losses, unless it has                       應收款項），則 本 集 團 不
                       incurred obligations or made payments on behalf                        會確認進一步虧損，除非
                       of the other entity.                                                   本集團已代其他實體承

                       Unrealised gains on transactions between the                           本集團與其聯營公司之

    3.7 Impairment of non-financial assets                               3.7   非金融資產之減值
         Goodwill and franchise rights that have an indefinite                 使用壽命不確定的商譽及特許
         useful life are not subject to amortisation and are                   經 營 權 毋 須 攤 銷，但 須 每 年 進
         tested annually for impairment, or more frequently if                 行 減 值 測 試，或 倘 有 事 件 或 情
         events or changes in circumstances indicate that they                 況 變 動 顯 示 可 能 減 值，則 更 頻
         might be impaired. Other assets are tested for                        密 地 進 行 減 值 測 試。倘 有 事 件
         impairment whenever events or changes in                              出現或情況改變顯示賬面值可
         circumstances indicate that the carrying amount may                   能 無 法 收 回，則 對 其 他 資 產 進
         not be recoverable. An impairment loss is recognised                  行 減 值 測 試。減 值 虧 損 按 資 產
         for the amount by which the asset’s carrying amount                   賬面值超出其可收回金額之差
         exceeds its recoverable amount. The recoverable                       額 確 認。可 收 回 金 額 是 指 資 產
         amount is the higher of an asset’s fair value less costs              公平值減去銷售成本後的餘額
         of disposal and value in use. For the purposes of                     和使用價值兩者中的較高者。
         assessing impairment, assets are grouped at the lowest                就 評 估 減 值 而 言，資 產 按 可 分
         levels for which there are separately identifiable cash               開識別現金流入之最低層次組
         inflows which are largely independent of the cash                     合，其 大 部 分 獨 立 於 其 他 資 產
         inflows from other assets or groups of assets (cash-                  或資產組別（現金產生單位）之
         generating units). Non-financial assets other than                    現金流入。除商譽外，已出現減
         goodwill that suffered an impairment are reviewed for                 值之非金融資產於各報告期末
         possible reversal of the impairment at the end of each                審查是否可能撥回減值。
         reporting period.

    3.8 Non-current assets held for sale                                 3.8   持有待售之非流動資產
         Non-current assets are classified as held for sale if                 倘非流動資產之賬面值可主要
         their carrying amount will be recovered principally                   通 過 銷 售 交 易，而 非 通 過 持 續
         through a sale transaction rather than through                        使 用 而 收 回，且 被 視 為 極 可 能
         continuing use and a sale is considered highly                        出 售 則 會 分 類 為 持 有 待 售。該
         probable. They are measured at the lower of their                     等資產乃按其賬面值及公平值
         carrying amount and fair value less costs to sell, except             減 銷 售 成 本 之 較 低 者 計 量，惟
         for assets such as deferred income tax assets, assets                 本要求明確豁免的遞延所得稅
         arising from employee benefits, financial assets and                  資產、僱員福利產生之資產、金
         investment property that are carried at fair value and                融資產及按公平值列賬之投資
         contractual rights under insurance contracts, which                   物業及保險合約下之合約權利
         are specifically exempt from this requirement.                        等資產則除外。

         An impairment loss is recognised for any initial or                   倘資產（或出售組別）於任何初
         subsequent write-down of the asset (or disposal group)                步或其後撇減至公平值減出售
         to fair value less costs to sell. A gain is recognised                成本，則確認減值虧損。倘其後
         for any subsequent increases in fair value less costs                 任何增加之公平值減出售資產
         to sell of an asset (or disposal group), but not in                   （或出售組別）之成本，則確認
         excess of any cumulative impairment loss previously                   收 益，惟 不 得 超 出 先 前 已 確 認
         recognised. A gain or loss not previously recognised                  之 任 何 累 計 減 值 虧 損。先 前 並
         by the date of the sale of the non-current asset (or                  未於出售非流動資產（或出售組
         disposal group) is recognised at the date of                          別）日期確認之收益或虧損於終
         derecognition.                                                        止確認日期確認。

    3.10 Offsetting financial instruments                               3.10 抵銷金融工具
         Financial assets and liabilities are offset and the net             當有法定可執行權利抵銷已確
         amount reported in the statement of financial position              認金額及有意按淨額基準結算
         when there is a legally enforceable right to offset the             或同時變現資產及結算負債
         recognised amounts and there is an intention to settle              時，金 融 資 產 及 負 債 可 互 相 抵
         on a net basis or realise the asset and settle the                  銷，並 在 財 務 狀 況 表 內 呈 報 淨
         liability simultaneously. The legally enforceable right             額。法 律 上 可 強 制 執 行 的 權 利
         must not be contingent on future events and must be                 不 得 依 賴 未 來 事 件 而 定，而 在
         enforceable in the normal course of business and in                 一般業務過程中以及倘本公司
         the event of default, insolvency or bankruptcy of the               或 對 手 方 一 旦 出 現 違 約、無 償
         Company or the counterparty.                                        債 能 力 或 破 產 時，也 必 須 可 強

    3.11 Inventories                                                    3.11 存貨
         Raw materials and finished goods are stated at the                  原材料及製成品按成本與可變

         the latter being allocated on the basis of normal                   開 支，後 者 按 正 常 營 運 能 力 分
         operating capacity. Cost excludes borrowing costs.                  配。成本不包括借貸成本。成本
         Costs are assigned to individual items of inventory on              按加權平均成本分配至個別存
         the basis of weighted average costs. Costs of purchased             貨 項 目。購 入 存 貨 之 成 本 於 扣
         inventory are determined after deducting rebates and                除 返 利 及 折 扣 後 釐 定。可 變 現
         discounts. Net realisable value is the estimated selling            淨值為日常業務過程中之估計

           3.13 Cash and cash equivalents                                         3.13 現金及現金等值項目
                For the purpose of presentation in the statement of                    就 呈 列 現 金 流 量 表 而 言，現 金
                cash flows, cash and cash equivalents includes cash                    及現金等值項目包括庫存現
                on hand, deposits held at call with financial institutions,            金、金融機構通知存款、原到期
                other short-term, highly liquid investments with original              日為三個月或以下可隨時轉換

                convertible to known amounts of cash and which are                     險不大之其他短期高流動性投
                subject to an insignificant risk of changes in value,                  資 及 銀 行 透 支。銀 行 透 支 於 財
                and bank overdrafts. Bank overdrafts are shown within                  務狀況表之流動負債內之借貸
                borrowings in current liabilities in the statement of                  中呈列。
                financial position.

                The Group’s cash and cash equivalents only includes                   本集團的現金及現金等值項目

           3.17 Current and deferred income tax                                3.17 即期及遞延所得稅
                The income tax expense or credit for the period is                  期內所得稅支出或抵免為本期
                the tax payable on the current period’s taxable income              應課稅收入項根據各司法轄區
                based on the applicable income tax rate for each                    適用之所得稅率計算之應繳稅
                jurisdiction, adjusted by changes in deferred income                項，並 經 暫 時 差 額 應 佔 遞 延 所
                tax assets and liabilities attributable to temporary                得稅資產及負債變動及未動用
                differences and to unused tax losses.                               稅項虧損調整。

                Current income tax                                                 即期所得稅
                The current income tax charge is calculated on the                 即期所得稅支出乃根據本公司
                basis of the tax laws enacted or substantively enacted             及其附屬公司及聯營公司經營
                at the end of the reporting period in the countries                及產生應課稅收入之國家於報
                where the company and its subsidiaries and associates              告期末已頒佈或實質頒佈之稅
                operate and generate taxable income. Management                    法 計 算。管 理 層 就 適 用 稅 務 法
                periodically evaluates positions taken in tax returns              例詮釋所規限之情況定期評
                with respect to situations in which applicable tax                 估 報 稅 表 之 狀 況，並 考 慮 稅 務
                regulation is subject to interpretation and considers              機關是否可能接受不確定之稅
                whether it is probable that a taxation authority will              務 處 理。本 集 團 根 據 最 可 能 之
                accept an uncertain tax treatment. The Group                       金額或預期價值計量其稅項結
                measures its tax balances either based on the most                 餘，視 乎 哪 種 方 法 能 為 解 決 不
                likely amount or the expected value, depending on                  確定性提供更好的預測。
                which method provides a better prediction of the
                resolution of the uncertainty.

                Deferred income tax                                                遞延所得稅
                Deferred income tax is provided in full, using the                 遞延所得稅採用負債法就資產
                liability method, on temporary differences arising                 及負債之稅基與其於綜合財務
                between the tax bases of assets and liabilities and                報表之賬面值間之暫時差額作
                their carrying amounts in the consolidated financial               出全數撥備。然而，倘遞延所得
                statements. However, deferred income tax liabilities               稅負債來自商譽之初步確認，
                are not recognised if they arise from the initial                  則 不 予 確 認。倘 遞 延 所 得 稅 來
                recognition of goodwill. Deferred income tax is also               自在交易（業務合併除外）中對
                not accounted for if it arises from initial recognition            資 產 或 負 債 的 初 步 確 認，而 在
                of an asset or liability in a transaction other than a             交易時不影響會計損益或應課
                business combination that at the time of the transaction           稅 損 益，且 並 無 產 生 等 額 應 課
                affects neither accounting nor taxable profit or loss              稅 及 可 扣 稅 暫 時 差 額，則 不 會
                and does not give rise to equal taxable and deductible             入 賬。遞 延 所 得 稅 採 用 於 報 告
                temporary differences. Deferred income tax is                      期 末 前 已 頒 佈 或 實 質 頒 佈，並
                determined using tax rates (and laws) that have been               預期於變現相關遞延所得稅資
                enacted or substantively enacted by the end of the                 產或償還遞延所得稅負債時適
                reporting period and are expected to apply when the                用之稅率（及法例）釐定。
                related deferred income tax asset is realised or the
                deferred income tax liability is settled.

    3.19 Revenue recognition                                            3.19 收入確認
         The Group recognises revenue when (or as) a                         於（或就此）完成履約義務時，
         performance obligation is satisfied, i.e. when “control”            本 集 團 確 認 收 入，即 於 特 定 履
         of the goods or services underlying the particular                  約義務的相關商品或服務的
         performance obligation is transferred to the customer.             「控制權」轉讓予客戶時確認。

         A performance obligation represents a good or service              履約義務指不同的商品及服務
         (or a bundle of goods or services) that is distinct or             （或一組商品或服務）或一系列
         a series of distinct goods or services that are                    大致相等的不同商品及服務。
         substantially the same.

         Control is transferred over time and revenue is                     控 制 權 隨 時 間 轉 移，而 倘 滿 足
         recognised over time by reference to the progress                   以 下 其 中 一 項 標 準，則 收 入 乃
         towards complete satisfaction of the relevant                       參照完全滿足相關履約義務的
         performance obligation if one of the following criteria             進展情況而隨時間確認：
         is met:

         •     the customer simultaneously receives and                      •    於本集團履約時，客戶同
               consumes the benefits provided by the Group’s                      時取得並耗用本集團履
               performance as the Group performs;                                 約所提供的利益；

         •     the Group’s performance creates or enhances                   •    本集團之履約產生或強
               an asset that the customer controls as the Group                   化一項資產，該資產於本
               performs; or                                                       集團履約時乃由客戶控
                                                                                  制；或

         •     the Group’s performance does not create an                    •    本集團的履約並未產生
               asset with an alternative use to the Group and                     對本集團有替代用途的
               the Group has an enforceable right to payment                      資產，且本集團對迄今已
               for performance completed to date.                                 完成履約之付款具有可
                                                                                  執行之權利。

         Otherwise, revenue is recognised at a point in time                 否 則，收 入 於 客 戶 獲 得 商 品 或
         when the customer obtains control of the distinct goods             服務控制權的時間點確認。
         or service.

           3.21 Leases                                                             3.21 租賃
                Leases are recognised as a right-of-use asset and a                     租賃於租賃資產可供本集團使
                corresponding liability at the date at which the leased                 用當日確認為使用權資產及相
                asset is available for use by the Group.                                應負債。

                Contracts may contain both lease and non-lease                        合約可包含租賃及非租賃部
                components. The Group allocates the consideration                     分。本 集 團 將 合 約 中 之 代 價 按
                in the contract to the lease and non-lease components                 租賃及非租賃部份之相對獨立
                based on their relative stand-alone prices. However,                  價 格 分 配。然 而，就 本 集 團 為
                for leases of real estate for which the Group is a                    承 租 人 之 房 地 產 租 賃 而 言，其
                lessee, it has elected not to separate lease and non-                 選擇不將租賃及非租賃部分分
                lease components and instead accounts for these as                    開，而 將 該 等 部 分 入 賬 為 單 一
                a single lease component.                                             租賃部分。

                Lease terms are negotiated on an individual basis and                 租賃條款乃按個別基準磋商，
                contain a wide range of different terms and conditions.               並包含廣泛的不同條款及條
                The lease agreements do not impose any covenants                      件。除 出 租 人 持 有 之 租 賃 資 產
                other than the security interests in the leased assets                之 擔 保 權 益 外，租 賃 協 定 並 無
                that are held by the lessor. Leased assets may not be                 附 加 任 何 契 諾。租 賃 資 產 不 得
                used as security for borrowing purposes.                              用作借款的擔保。

                Assets and liabilities arising from a lease are initially             來自租賃之資產及負債初步按
                measured on a present value basis. Lease liabilities include          現 值 基 準 計 量。租 賃 負 債 包 括
                the net present value of the following lease payments:                下列租賃付款之淨現值：

                •      fixed payments (including in-substance fixed                   •   固定付款（包括實質上是
                       payments), less any lease incentives receivable;                   固定之付款）減任何應收
                                                                                          租賃優惠；

                •      variable lease payments that are based on an                   •   取決於生效之日初始計
                       index or a rate, initially measured using the index                量所使用的指數或比率
                       or rate as at the commencement date.                               之可變租賃付款。

         a similar economic environment with similar terms,                    用權資產具有類似價值的資產
         security and conditions.                                              所需的利率。

         To determine the incremental borrowing rate, the                     為 釐 定 借 貸 利 率 之 遞 增，本 集
         Group:                                                               團：

               individual lessee as a starting point, adjusted to                  的第三方融資為起點，調

               third party financing was received.                                 融資以來融資條件的變

         loss over the lease period so as to produce a constant               自 損 益 扣 除，以 就 各 期 間 之 負
         periodic rate of interest on the remaining balance of                債結餘得出固定定期利率。
         the liability for each period.

   The Group gives credit term to key customers, which granted           本集團授予關鍵客戶信貸期，信貸期
   with credit term ranging from 7 to 90 days upon delivery.             為交貨後的7至90天。而對於其他客
   While for other customers, payment in advance or payment              戶則通常須預收款項或於交貨時收
   on delivery is normally required. The Group seeks to maintain         款。本集團尋求維持嚴格控制其未償
   strict control over its outstanding receivables and has a credit      還應收賬款及設有信貸控制專員，務
   control commissioner to minimise credit risk. Overdue                 求將信貸風險減至最低。高級管理層
   balances are reviewed regularly by senior management. In              定期審核逾期結餘。鑒於本集團涉及
   view of the fact that the Group’s trade receivables relate to         大量不同客戶之應收貿易賬款，故信
